=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
There are N buildings, Building 1, Building 2, \ldots, Building N, arranged in a line in this order. The height of Building i (1 \leq i \leq N) is H_i.
For each i = 1, 2, \ldots, N, find the number of integers j (i < j \leq N) satisfying the following condition:

- There is no building taller than Building j between Buildings i and j.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
H_1 H_2 \ldots H_N

Output

For each i = 1, 2, \ldots, N, let c_i be the number of j satisfying the condition. Print c_1, c_2, \ldots, c_N in order, separated by spaces.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq H_i \leq N
-  H_i\neq H_j\ (i\neq j)
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5
2 1 4 3 5

Sample Output 1

3 2 2 1 0

For i=1, the integers j satisfying the condition are 2, 3, and 5: there are three. (Between Buildings 1 and 4, there is a building taller than Building 4, which is Building 3, so j=4 does not satisfy the condition.) Therefore, the first number in the output is 3.

Sample Input 2

4
1 2 3 4

Sample Output 2

3 2 1 0

Sample Input 3

10
1 9 6 5 2 7 10 4 8 3

Sample Output 3

2 3 3 3 2 1 2 1 1 0

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

stack = []
for i in range(1,N+1):
    h=H[i-1]

bit = [0]*(N+2)  # indices 1..N+1
def add(idx, val):
    while idx <= N+1:
       bit[idx]+=val; idx += idx & -idx
def sum(idx):
    s=0
    while idx>0:
       s+=bit[idx]; idx-=idx&-idx
    return s
ans=[0]*(N+1)
for i in range(N,0,-1):
    ans[i] = sum(i+1)  # active j > i, count p_j <= i
    add(p[i]+1,1)
print(' '.join(map(str, ans[1:])))

Let's consider memory and speed. N=2e5, Python O(N log N) fine. Need maybe optimize I/O. Use sys.stdin.buffer.read. Code:

import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N = data[0]
    H = data[1:]
    p = [0]*(N+1)
    st = []
    for i, h in enumerate(H, start=1):
        while st and H[st[-1]-1] < h:
            st.pop()
        p[i] = st[-1] if st else 0
        st.append(i)
    size = N + 2
    bit = [0]*(size+1) # if size N+2, indices up to N+1? Let's define size=N+1 maybe.
    def add(idx, val):
        while idx <= size:
            bit[idx] += val
            idx += idx & -idx
    def sum_prefix(idx):
        s=0
        while idx>0:
            s += bit[idx]
            idx -= idx & -idx
        return s
    ans = [0]*(N+1)
    for i in range(N,0,-1):
        ans[i] = sum_prefix(i+1)
        add(p[i]+1, 1)
    sys.stdout.write(' '.join(map(str, ans[1:])))

if __name__ == "__main__": main()

For each j, valid i are p_j <= i < j, where p_j is the nearest index to the left with height > H_j (0 if none). Because:

Now, range-add implementation:
p = [0]*(N+1)
st = []
for i in range(1,N+1):
    h = H[i-1]
    while st and H[st[-1]-1] < h:
        st.pop()
    p[i] = st[-1] if st else 0
    st.append(i)

diff = [0]*(N+2)  # indices 0..N+1 maybe
for j in range(2, N+1):  # j=1 no valid i
    l = p[j]
    if l == 0:
        l = 1
    # r = j-1
    diff[l] += 1
    diff[j] -= 1  # since interval ends at j-1, prefix stops before j
# prefix
ans = []
cur = 0
for i in range(1,N+1):
    cur += diff[i]
    ans.append(str(cur))
print(' '.join(ans))

Now, final code can be:

import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]
    H = data[1:1+N]
    prev = [0]*(N+1)
    st = []
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        h = H[i-1]
        while st and H[st[-1]-1] < h:
            st.pop()
        if st:
            prev[i] = st[-1]
        st.append(i)

    diff = [0]*(N+2)
    for j in range(2, N+1):
        l = prev[j]
        if l == 0:
            l = 1
        diff[l] += 1
        diff[j] -= 1

    ans = []
    cur = 0
    for i in range(1, N+1):
        cur += diff[i]
        ans.append(str(cur))
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]
    H = [0] + data[1:1+N]

    prev = [0]*(N+1)
    st = []
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        h = H[i]
        while st and H[st[-1]] < h:
            st.pop()
        if st:
            prev[i] = st[-1]
        st.append(i)

    diff = [0]*(N+2)
    for j in range(2,N+1):
        l = prev[j]
        if l == 0:
            l = 1
        diff[l] += 1
        diff[j] -= 1

    ans = []
    cur = 0
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        cur += diff[i]
        ans.append(str(cur))
    print(" ".join(ans))
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Now, let's maybe think if there is an even more compact code:
```
import sys
def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N = int(input())
    H = [0] + list(map(int, input().split()))
    diff = [0]*(N+2)
    st = []
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        h = H[i]
        while st and H[st[-1]] <= h:
            st.pop()
        p = st[-1] if st else 0
        if i > 1:
            diff[p if p else 1] += 1
            diff[i] -= 1
        st.append(i)
    ans = []
    cur = 0
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        cur += diff[i]
        ans.append(str(cur))
    print(' '.join(ans))
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
This uses `diff[p if p else 1]`. If p=0 and i>1, l=1. If p>=1, l=p. Good.
Potential issue: If p=0 and i=1 skip; if i>1, diff[1]. Good.
If p=0 and i=2, diff[1]+=1. Good.
If p=0 and i=N, diff[1]+=1,diff[N]-=1. Good.

diff same. Good.

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return

    N = data[0]
    H = [0] + data[1:1 + N]

    diff = [0] * (N + 2)
    stack = []

    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        h = H[i]

        while stack and H[stack[-1]] <= h:
            stack.pop()

        p = stack[-1] if stack else 0

        if i >= 2:
            l = p if p != 0 else 1
            diff[l] += 1
            diff[i] -= 1

        stack.append(i)

    ans = []
    cur = 0
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        cur += diff[i]
        ans.append(str(cur))

    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
